營收走勢圖

研究員/蔡佾璋 EASONTSAI@uni-psg.com

研究部維持南亞(1303)之投資建議為買進，主要考量：(1)南亞 7
月自結稅後 EPS 0.4 元，雖不如 6 月自結稅後 EPS 2.88 元，但因
6 月有包含權益法認列收益不具可比性，而具可比性的 4、5、7 月
自結稅前淨利率分別是 9.4%、13%、15.7%，歸母淨利率為 4.7%、
9.2%、10.4%，獲利顯著持續提升，符合研究部預期。營收方面，
7+8 月營收達成率 70.6%，優於預期，公司展望 9 月營收將優於 8
月，4Q26 的營收將優於 3Q26，整體營運維持正向。(2)應用於 AI
伺服器的 M6-M8 高階大訂單目前正與客戶洽談生產配合及廠區產
線對接等細節，預計將於 4Q26 完成協商並開始有明顯進展，帶動
後續營收與利潤大幅優化，去瓶頸後的 30 萬張 CCL 產能將全數投
入高階產品生產。(3)廠商持續將擴產資源湧入中高階材料，導致
以 PCB 為主軸的玻纖布、銅箔、CCL、ABF 載板等中低階電子材料
依然緊缺，研究部預期南亞電子材料漲價態勢將延續。此外，南亞
積極推動 AI 供應鏈高階材料的開發、認證與銷售，有顯著成效，
2027 年電子相關獲利占比也將提升至 98%，電子材料占比可望達
70-80%，優於研究部原先預估的65-70%。(4)子公司方面，受惠AI
需求旺盛 ， 南電產能有限但需求持續放大 ，ABF 載板漲價效應延續 ，
2026、2027 年業績皆會有明顯提升；南亞科受惠記憶體供需缺口
再擴大，利潤急速成長。(5)近期石化產品 EG 價格大漲，利差顯著
擴大，對獲利明顯幫助，若市況持續好轉，更明顯的獲利貢獻會落
在 4Q26。研究部分別上修南亞 2026 年、2027 年稅後 EPS 至 13.82
元、21.64 元(原估 12.02 元、16.94 元)，分別+2,325%YoY、
+56.6%YoY，年底淨值 69.8 元、91.4 元。研究部認為 2027 年 CSP
業者對整機成本及零組件價格控管趨嚴 ， 加上上游原料新增產能於
2H27 至1H28 陸續開出，電子材料及CCL 業者議價能力會降低，因
此將電子材料目標評價調降至 20 倍，惟考量目前股價隱含電子材
料評價僅約 12 倍，評價極低，故維持南亞買進投資建議。以2027
年電子材料:南亞科:傳產獲利占比 48:50:2，電子與傳產淨值占比
約 4：6 之比例推算，目標價調升至320 元(20xPER*2027 本業電子
材料 EPS + 5xPER*2027 南亞科貢獻稅後 EPS + 1xPBR*2027 傳產
BVPS)。

重點摘要： 
1. 南亞產品線包括在石化、塑膠、纖維與電子類，分佈廣泛

圖 1、南亞 1H26 部門別營收比重 
電子材料
52.7%
化工產品
16.9%
聚酯產品
15.8%
塑膠加工
12.7%
其他
1.9%

資料來源：公司、統一投顧整理

南亞是全球最大塑膠加工廠，此外，其電子級玻纖絲為全球
第二大 ， 銅箔基板 、 玻纖布為全球第三大 ， 乙二醇EG為全球第
四大，環氧樹脂、丙二酚BPA為全球第五大業者。南亞產能全
球布局，主要生產基地在台灣，有57個廠， 中國有34個， 美國
有8個廠，共99個生產廠。

同時，南亞持有 26.3%的南亞科為全球第四大 DRAM廠，持有
61.4%的子公司南電為 「PCB三雄」 之一 ， 穩居全球前五大IC載
板廠，也是全球前十大PCB製造商。南亞的產品線為台塑集團
中最廣泛，分佈在石化、 纖維 、塑膠加工及電子材料等，1H26
電子材料佔比由 2025年的46.4% 提升到52.7%，化工產品由
21.8%降至16.9%，聚酯產品由16%略降至15.8%，塑膠產品由
13.9%降至12.7%， 其他1.9%。 公司生產的原材料多為集團內部
自用 ， 僅約少部分對外銷售 ， 外售若對公司更有幫助的話 ， 公
司將會提高外售比率。

聚酯產品包括聚酯薄膜、聚酯纖維(如聚酯棉、POY、DTY以及
瓶用酯粒)等 。 電子材料產品包括銅箔 、 銅箔基板(CCL)、 玻纖
絲、玻纖布、環氧樹脂(EPOXY)等；塑膠產品包括膠布、膠皮
與PU合成皮等。銷售地區廣泛，以中國、美國、台灣為主。

2. 子公司南電、南亞科漲價趨勢延續，母公司南亞間接受惠 
由於AI應用需求大增 ，ASIC需求預估在2026年大幅增加 ， 此類
產品全都用ABF載板 ，ABF載板於4Q25起漲 ，1Q26及2Q26皆維持
續漲價節奏。研究部認為接下來ABF廠議價能力轉強，南電報
價得以持續上漲，母公司南亞間接受惠。

南亞科2Q26持續繳出亮眼的成績，GM及OPM分別達到79.5%及
73.7%的水準 ， 不亞於一線大廠 ， 單季稅後EPS 14.66元 。 南亞
科預期3Q26營運及OPM將進一步提升 ， 且短缺情況至4Q26未見
變化 ， 同時預期市場在2028年前不會發生反轉 ， 母公司南亞間
接受惠。

股權變動方面，董事會之前決議處分南電 3%與南亞科1%的股
權 ， 其中 ， 南電的第一個3%已經處分完畢 ， 後來董事會又通過
再處分3%， 大部分已於2Q26底處分完畢 ， 目前沒有新的處分計
畫，兩次重大處分後仍持有南電61%股權。

### Page 4

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  4

2026/3/25南亞科董事會宣布晟碟 （Sandisk） 、 鎧俠 （Kioxia）、
SK Hynix旗下的Solidigm、思科（Cisco）四家公司分三次私
募認購普通股共3.5億股 ， 股權稀釋約11.3%,研究部推算目前
南亞持有南亞科之持股比例約為26.3%。 而南亞持有南亞科的
3,099萬股權處分目前尚未執行，年底前也不一定會處分，預
估執行後持股比例將調整至25.4%。

3. 電子材料營收自1H26的52.7%提升至9月的60%以上

表1、電子材料為南亞獲利主體(營業利益佔比) 
資料來源：公司、統一投顧整理

缺貨造成電子材料持續漲價，2025年初以來最先出現明顯供
給吃緊的玻纖 ， 近期已擴散至銅箔 、 銅箔基板等材料 ， 各材料
缺貨、漲價還會延續一段時間。中低階產品缺貨主因在於高
階化帶來的產能排擠，隨著玻纖、銅箔及基板高階產品製程
精度提升，生產速度下降，不論銅箔、玻纖、基板，要生產1
單位的高階材料，都必須放棄生產很多單位的中低階材料，
導致缺貨更嚴重，此情況將維持未來一段時間。

電子材料持續成為南亞營運轉型核心，產線維持高稼動率，
銅箔基板、玻纖布與玻纖絲產能利用率達滿載，銅箔與環氧
樹脂產能利用率在8-9成之間。8月電子材料營收占比已提升
至59.8%，較7月增加2.8pct， 正式逼近六成 。 電子材料產業到
2028、2029年仍可維持榮景。

業界有開始擴產，但擴產速度本身有限制，且擴產後也還是
會面臨材料的問題，此外，PCB層數大幅增加，將使得供給增
加，幅度仍跟不上需求的增速 。 整體而言 ， 電子材料供需偏緊
與漲價趨勢短期難以扭轉 ， 銅箔基板及高階PCB的浪潮會有望
延續。

### Page 5

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  5

4. 玻纖獲利率最高，獲利貢獻CCL最大，全品項OPM持續提升 
表 2、南亞各產品別 OPM 逐季轉佳 
資料來源：公司、統一投顧整理

南亞產品價格漲幅因客戶依賴度而異，依客戶每個月機動調
整 。 由於中高階產品供不應求 ， 加上同業將產能轉做高階 ， 導
致一般消費性電子用的E-Glass產能吃緊 ， 這對產品報價相當
有利，報價自2025年開始每個月約調漲5-10%。

獲利率方面，現階段電子材料獲利率以玻纖紗/布排行第一、
近期CCL跟載板漲幅度大，獲利率已逐漸接近玻纖，排名升至
第二，再來是銅箔，而環氧樹脂有8成為非電子用途，因此獲
利率較銅箔低些。

獲利金額貢獻方面，雖然目前獲利率最高的是玻纖紗/布，但
CCL營收規模較大，所以獲利貢獻金額超過玻纖。

持續漲價對3Q26、4Q26利潤率是很重要因素，但除了漲價之
外 ， 產能利用率拉高 ， 固定成本不變 ， 因此訂單及出貨量增加
對利潤率也有一定貢獻， 產品組合改變也很重要， 整體來說 ，
電子材料全產品項的OPM皆持續提升當中。

電子材料產品主要包括： 
I.玻纖布 
南亞的玻纖布產能排名為全球第三，佔南亞的營收提升至 6-
7%，但獲利貢獻位居前列。年產能約5.9億米(以一般布計)，
自用比重原為40%-50%， 但為優先確保下游高階CCL供料無虞 ，
外銷比例已調降至30%-40%(自用仍以市價計價，不影響實質
利潤)，稼動率自6月起持續滿載。受全球嚴重缺料帶動，7-9
月玻纖布價格強勁上揚，預期4Q26動能不減。玻纖布/紗獲利
率為電子材料中最高，2Q26 OPM已達30%-40%以上。

### Page 6

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  6

日系知名同業大廠為玻纖紗 /布領導廠商，將專注於高階市
場，而Low DK1玻纖布市場供不應求 ， 進而尋找南亞進行合作 。
此日本同業大廠提供玻纖紗，而南亞可提供Low DK1產能，製
成玻纖布後再回送日廠做後段處理。

此外，日本同業大廠也有供應Low DK2玻纖紗給南亞，織成布
後製成自家CCL基板可對外銷售，Low DK2 產品預計2026年內
會量產 ， 屬長期技術互惠合作 。 初期量不大 ， 但3Q26起日東紡
供貨量將明顯增加，足以支撐公司M8以上高階基板需求，對
於公司後續CCL發展，以及高階玻纖數量擴大會有一定幫助。

E-Glass部分 ， 南亞表示各類電子與消費型載板 、PCB層數持續
升級，即使終端消費總量持平，對E-Glass的用料需求依然持
續擴大。E-Glass自2025年底起漲勢確立，3Q26價格仍持續上
漲，公司在9月會議中表示，中國同業於年中報價約2.2美元/
米，4Q26期貨已漲至2.5美元/米 ， 公司預期2027年有機會上看
3美元/米，漲價動能依然非常強勁。南亞E-Glass採大窯爐生
產，與特殊玻纖的坩堝製程不衝突。

同業因原料緊缺或利潤考量放棄中低階訂單，客戶大量轉向
原料掌握度極高的南亞，使南亞在中階與消費型基板 (如M4
等)的稼動率維持滿載 ， 南亞不會放棄E-Glass供應 ， 未來將憑
藉原料自給優勢，承接大量同業外溢轉單，充分享受價量齊
揚帶來的超額利潤。

特殊玻纖紗使用白金坩堝設備，包含坩堝設計、冷卻拉絲等
環節有許多細微變化，會影響產品的介電常數。全球高階豐
田織布機排單已至2028-2029年 ， 南亞因為有超前部署織布機
產能 ， 提早下訂排單 ， 自4Q26起每年均有新機台如期到位加入
生產。透過台灣新港與昆山廠增設坩堝擴產，2026-2028年兩
岸累計將新增達100多個紡位。

高階石英布（Q-Glass）南亞在技術上仍需突破，初期石英紗
會先從外部供應商取得 ， 但因為公司具備生產玻纖紗的經驗 ，
目前還沒放棄，持續積極投入自主研發。

### Page 7

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  7

II.銅箔 
南亞的銅箔產能排名全球第二，近期銅箔佔南亞營收已超過
10%。銅箔年產能約為11萬多噸，目前產能利用率滿載，自用
比例約50%。公司計畫在惠州擴建銅箔產線，將增加2.34萬噸
產能 ， 約擴張2成 ， 預計於2028年1月投產 。 台灣彰濱地區也有
銅箔擴產規劃，主要針對PCB應用為主。

南亞的銅箔目前銷量以HVLP1/2為主 ，HVLP3穩定量產 ， 且具備
HVLP 3.5(介於3與4之間的自有規格)及小量HVLP 4生產能力 ，
另外也有一些特定規格之超薄銅箔已進入量產，HVLP 5 則是
持續開發當中，產品線正積極朝向高階發展。

AI伺服器對超低損耗要求嚴格，高階銅箔結晶晶粒需極細，
製程具物理極限使生產速度大幅變慢，投下相同資源，高階
產出噸數遠低於傳統規格，產生嚴重產能排擠效應。

加工費方面，過去多逐季協商、具價格僵固性， 但自7、8月起
開始出現密集且連續調漲 。3Q26大宗銅箔每噸售價約1.7-1.8
萬美元(含銅價約1.4萬)，加工費約3-4千美元/噸。因結構性
生產降速與高階需求不可逆，預期加工費將具備永久性上升
動能，後續極有機會突破4-5千美元/噸。

III.銅箔基板(CCL) 
南亞的銅箔基板 （CCL） 產能排名全球第三 ， 目前CCL兩岸產能
約618萬張/月，台灣 ：中國崑山與惠州約3：7， 目前稼動率滿
載，公司表示1H26 M4 以上基板銷量逾200萬張。台灣樹林與
新港廠區既有設備的小幅改造去瓶頸進度超前，已提前於
4Q26初實現30萬張產能提升 ， 約+5%YoY， 將全數投入M7以上高
階訂單。

CCL佔南亞的營收比重已於 7月達30%左右，M系列佔其中的
2/3(約整體營收20%)，M4佔M系列的營收60%(約CCL營收40%，
約整體營收12%)，其他規格(M6至M8)則是整體營收8%。

### Page 8

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  8

南亞CCL具備M4至M9等級量產能力，其中，M4是出貨量最大產
品，主要供應NVIDIA用於RTX 50 系列顯卡。1H26 M6-M8處於
客戶開發與送樣階段 ， 與多位知名終端客戶與CSP客戶積極洽
談大單，預計4Q26協商將陸續完成，並將迎來明顯放量(合約
會要求客戶提供保障資金)，產品組合升級亦帶動營收、獲利
明顯提升 。M9目前則是已具量產能力並進入客戶認證測試中 ，
數據回饋理想且有極小量試賣，預計大批量產時程將落在  
1H27。

CCL自4Q25起CCL每月維持3-5%漲幅，7、8月部分客戶補漲達
10-15%，4Q26將延續逐月調漲步調 。 南亞不放棄利潤依然可觀
且客戶轉單持續湧入的M4，採 「高中低階全線掌握」 ，藉由產
品組合優化大幅推升CCL獲利率 （2Q26 OPM近20%，3Q、4Q26將
進一步追趕目前玻纖的40% OPM水準）。

儘管目前台光電在M9市佔率最高，然南亞的策略是透過開發
M10等高階產品來實現「彎道超車」，一舉超越競爭對手。南
亞的M10採用自製配方，即K2特殊玻纖布搭配HVP4銅箔，以及
自研的碳氫樹脂，不需仰賴昂貴且進口申請繁瑣的石英布方
案，在未來的成本控制與產能擴張上，將具備極佳的競爭優
勢。M10目前則是還在認證中，實測Dk/Df數據已達M10理想水
準且優於同業 ， 因測試需時1-2千小時 。 整體認證持續推進中 ，
主控權在客戶端 ， 希望1H27取得更具體的驗證成果 ， 若認證順
利通過的話，2H27將逐漸放量。

5. 預估3Q26稅後EPS 4.28元，+942.7%YoY 
受惠於AI發展帶動中高階電子材料供應緊缺、價量齊揚，且
中東戰事帶動化工、聚酯、塑膠加工等產品售價隨原料行情
飆升 ， 南亞2Q26營收合計836.5億元 ，+21.9%QoQ，+27.3%YoY，
營收達成率97%， 符合預期 。 營業利益113.6億元 ，+203.5%QoQ，
+772%YoY， 主要受惠於AI產業擴大投資 ， 相關材料需求持續成
長，使電路板等電子材料產品利潤提高。而權益法投資(台塑
化、南亞科、台朔重工、麥寮汽電、台塑烯烴(美國))共認列
獲利190.9億元(原估145億元)， 優於預期 。2Q26歸屬母公司淨
利267.5億元(原估183.2億元)， 獲利達成率146%， 優於預期 ，
稅後EPS 3.37元(原估2.31元)，+87.7%QoQ，YoY虧轉盈。

展望2H26，雖預期3Q26聚酯部門因紡織用途市況不佳 ， 營收看
季減 ， 化工部門營收看持平 ， 然而 ， 塑膠加工部門營收穩健季
增，且公司看好AI算力規模於未來幾年內仍會大幅成長，邊
緣運算將帶動終端電子材料全系列產品升級，高階材料銷售
比重逐漸提高，電子材料將帶動南亞整體營收將持續向上攀
升，預期9月營運表現將優於8月，3Q26優於2Q26，4Q26優於
3Q26。。研究部預估3Q26營收924.3億元(原估877.9億元)，
+10.5%QoQ ，+44%YoY ， 營 業 利 益144.3 億元， +27.1%QoQ ，
+1,293%YoY ，稅後淨利 339.5 億元 ( 原估 268.4 億元 ) ，
+26.9%QoQ，+943%YoY，稅後EPS 4.28元(原估3.38元)。

6. 預估 2026 、2027 年稅後 EPS 13.82 元、 21.64 元 ， 分 別
+2,325%YoY、+56.6%YoY  
展望未來，研究部認為南亞電子材料事業持續增溫，公司整
體營運將進一步轉佳。預估 2026年營收3,416.8億元(原估
3,287.9億元)，+31.5%YoY，毛利率20.4%，+12.1pctYoY。全
年營業利益478.3億元，+1,192%YoY，本業營運明顯回升，加
上權益法認列轉投資子公司獲利大增 ， 預估2026全年稅後EPS 
13.82元(原估12.02元)，+2,325%YoY。

預估2027年營收4,533億元(原估3,788億元)，+32.7%YoY，毛
利率30.9%，+10.5pctYoY。全年營業利益1,099.5億元(原估
922億元)，+129.8%YoY， 本業營運持續提升 ， 權益法認列轉投
資子公司獲利進一步擴大 ， 預估2027全年稅後淨利1,717億元
(原估1,344億元)，+56.6%YoY，EPS 21.64元(原估16.94元)。

公司簡介 
亞德客成立於 1989 年，2009 年設立於開
曼群島的亞德客國際為控股主體 ， 是一家專
業生產各類氣動器材的集團 ， 公司主要從事
生產氣動控制元件 、 氣動執行原件 、 氣源處
理元件及氣動輔助元件之製造及銷售等業
務，以 「AirTAC」 自有品牌銷售於大中華地
區，中國市場佔營收 95％，台灣佔 2％、
海外佔 3％ ， 現為大陸市佔排名第二的氣動
元件廠，市佔率為30％，SMC 在中國市佔
率排名第一，達 33％。

主要客戶：電子業等 
主要競爭對手：SMC、Festo

林其美 iris.lin@sinopac.com 
本次報告：法說會

亞德客-KY (1590 TT) 
獲利依舊在成長軌道上

永豐觀點 
自動化行業景氣持續回溫，伴隨公司市占率提升或推出新品，2026、2027 年獲利
依舊在成長軌道上。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持買進建議：理由包括：(1) 氣動元件 2026、2027 年仍處於上升週期，預估公
司營收皆將保持雙位數成長，此得益於中國政府政策激勵與公司市占率持續提升，
(2) 2026 年線軌市場景氣逐漸回溫，預計年底時公司稼動率可達五成，(3) 電控與
電動缸產品開始少量貢獻營收，公司將陸續推出新產品，開始累積客戶關係。評價
方面，公司本益比區間 20-40 倍，自動化行業景氣持續回溫，伴隨公司市占率提
升或推出新品 ，2026、2027 年獲利在成長軌道上 ， 維持買進建議 ， 目標價維持1972
元（27X 2027EPS）。

ESG 評析 
亞德客企業永續評鑑整體為 A 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向
平均得分分別為 A 等與 B+等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2024 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學) 
註 1：資誠永續發展服務股份有限公司僅於ＥＳＧ評鑑系統方法學建置過程中，就評分指標提供
專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用
本評估報告之第三方負任何責任。

亞德客為中國第二大氣動元件廠商：亞德客成立於 1989 年，2009 年設立於開曼
群島的亞德客國際為控股主體 ， 是一家專業生產各類氣動器材的集團， 公司主要從
事生產氣動控制元件、 氣動執行原件、 氣源處理元件及氣動輔助元件之製造及銷售
等業務，以「AirTAC」自有品牌銷售於大中華地區，中國市場佔營收 95％，台灣
佔 2％、海外佔3％，現為大陸市佔排名第二的氣動元件廠，市佔率為30％，SMC
在中國市佔率排名第一，達 33％。 
2026、2027 年仍處於景氣上升週期，2026 年全年營收將成長 20%以上，2027
年亦有雙位數成長：過去氣動元件 2 年一個上升週期，2 年一個下降週期，本波
2025 年開始進入上升週期，趨勢較溫和，預計成長可超過三年。2022-2024 年行
業雙位數衰退，2025 低個位數成長、預估 2026 將高個位數成長，而 2027 年中
位數成長。公司規格持續擴增、市占率提升，公司增長比行業多 10%，公司對下
半年與明年成長的預期來自於客戶回饋、中國政府政策支持 （包括十五五計畫，除
製造業產業升級外，養老與醫護亦有著墨） ，對未來自動化有激勵作用。行業應用
別當中 ， 電子業電子行業前八月營收成長20%， 電子業中25%-30%與智慧手機有
關，主要客人在產品上市前 3-6 個月拉貨，預估客戶一般規格新手機明年上半年
上市，故公司七月與八月手機成長超過兩成，可延續至第四季，下半年電子行業也
維持 20%成長；電池行業中國政府有新政策，公司營收開始逐月下降，但年成長
仍有三成，預估明年該行業仍會成長，因客戶在海外擴充產能；汽車行業公司市占
率僅 10%-20%，市占率持續提升下，明年雙位數成長；傳統行業如食品、包裝、
工具機等受惠於政府補貼政策 ， 客戶積極擴充自動化產能，下半年仍維持雙位數成
長；太陽能行業，有機會衰退減少或轉為正增長；半導體受惠於中國國產化政策，
過去每月營收 500-600 萬元，目前1300-1400 萬元，2027 年新規格上市，4Q26
可讓客戶開始認證。 
線軌市場逐漸回溫 ， 預計年底時稼動率可達五成 ：線軌 2025、2026 年出貨量增長
20%，今年市場螺桿大幅調漲價格，線軌穩定價格，公司持續提升出貨量，目前稼
動率 40%，預估年底時可達 50%，屆時毛利率可達 30%，稼動率 80%時，毛利率
40%。公司預估今年營收可達 6.5-7 億元人民幣，規劃十年後年營收可達 30 億元
人民幣。 
跨入電控與電動缸領域，已開始貢獻營收：電控系統中國市場規模 1000 億元人民
幣，但有很多不同系統，公司會挑市場規模大的產品跨入，該產品資本支出低但毛
利率高。過去幾年利用五個系列產品在去年時營收達 4 億元人民幣，主要是搭配
氣缸銷售傳感器，公司自行設計功能佳、成本低的晶片，今年將再推出3 個系列，
公司預估未來 10 年公司有機會做到 30 億元人民幣年營收。電動缸產品目前公司
積極開發 servo motor、driver，主要搭配氣動元件銷售，2028-2029 有機會直接
應用在終端產品。目前市場規模 100 億元人民幣，隨著機器人市場興起規模會再
擴大 ， 中國servo motor 產品品質穩定 、 價格很低 ， 不排除公司直接買小型motor，
規劃 2027 年下半年上市小型電動缸。 
財務預估：3Q26 公司在大陸與台灣均進行明顯的調薪，將影響該季營業利益率，
預估將低於 2Q26，預估該季 EPS 為 15.23 元；去年第四季基期較高，不過預估今
年第四季營收仍將維持正成長，公司維持全年營收成長 20%以上，營業利益率維
持 34%的指引。預估 2026 年合併營收為 437.58 億元(+27.46％YoY)，稅後淨利
118.43 億元(+40.99％YoY)，EPS 為 59.22 元。預估 2027 年合併營收為 526.51
億元(+20.32％YoY)，稅後淨利 143.59 億元(+21.24％YoY)，EPS 為 71.79 元。

營收走勢圖

電子–通信網路  
全新(2455.TT) 
產能不是問題，基板供應改善推升營收獲利成長

研究部初次給予全新買進建議，主因為：(1)InP 基板供應改善帶
動光電子快速放量，且高毛利光電子占比由 1H26 約 26%提升至 7 月
40%、8 月 46.1%，光電子超越微電子的黃金交叉將近；(2)產品組合轉
佳 、 較高毛利的客供料占比逐步提升及價格調整效益帶動獲利改善 ， 管
理層預期 3Q26 毛利率可回升至 40%以上；(3)展望 2027 年，美系客戶
200G PD 預計 1Q27 開始明顯放量，CW Laser 及 EML 發射端則接續放
量，管理層預期2027 年發射端營收有望超越接收端。綜上所述，研究
部預估全新2026/2027 營收為49.02/78.77 億元 ， 分別年增45%/60.7%，
EPS 分別為 6.08/11.63 元，評價方面，考量目前上游磊晶需求持續強
勁，且基板供應逐步改善有望帶動營收獲利成長，因此給予60 倍本益
比，目標價 700 元(60PER*2027EPS)。

1. 3Q26 基板供貨穩定帶動高毛利光電子營收佔比提升 
研究部預估3Q26 營收將季增 44.2%達13.92 億元，EPS 為1.69 元，
主係:(1)基板供應穩定加上客戶自行攜帶基板數量增加；(2)高毛
利光電子營收佔比持續提升 。 預估毛利率達42.3%， 季增3.7ppts，
短期營運動能持續向上。

2. MOCVD 產能持續擴充，產能不成問題，2027 年光電子成長可期 
7 月整體MOCVD 稼動率僅約 50%，2027 年GaAs 及InP 共用的MOCVD
機台數新增至 66 台，後續有望隨 InP 需求，調整成 InP 產能使
用，滿足光電子產品後續需求。展望 2027 年，接收端 200G PD 於
1Q27 開始放量，發射端 CW Laser 及 EML 於 1H27 開始量產，且發
射端有望超越接收端，成為 2027 年光電子營收成長動能。

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 2

公司簡介 
全新光電（2455）成立於1996 年，為全球主要Ⅲ-V 族化合物半導體專業磊
晶廠，以MOCVD（金屬有機化學氣相沉積）為核心技術，主要提供客戶磊晶
晶圓 ， 產品可分為微電子及光電子兩大領域 。 微電子產品以GaAs HBT、PHEMT
等磊晶片為主，主要應用於手機、Wi-Fi、基地台等射頻功率放大器 （PA），
目前為全球最大 GaAs PA 磊晶供應商；光電子產品則涵蓋 InGaAs PD/APD、
FP/DFB Laser 及 VCSEL 等磊晶片，應用於光通訊、資料中心、LiDAR 及感測
等市場。2Q26 產品組合為微電子 73%、光電子 27%。

圖一、全新 2Q26 營收佔比

資料來源：全新，統一投顧整理

產業概況 
(1) 手機進入成長高原期，WiFi7/8 與新興終端擴大GaAs 應用場景 
GaAs憑藉高頻、高線性度及高功率效率等特性，長期為無線通訊RF前端的
重要材料，主要應用於PA、LNA及部分Switch，其中HBT仍為手機與WiFi PA
的主流技術。過去GaAs需求主要隨智慧型手機通訊規格升級而成長，隨4G
進入5G後，新增頻段使PA數量及RF前端複雜度同步提升，5G手機所需GaAs
磊晶面積約較4G增加20%～30%，帶動單機GaAs價值持續提高。

然而，隨全球智慧型手機市場逐步成熟，未來GaAs需求成長動能將由過去
依賴手機出貨量增加 ， 轉向無線通訊規格升級與連網終端數量擴張 。 其中 ，
WiFi 7為目前最明確的新增需求來源，除手機、PC及Router持續升級外，
應用範圍亦逐步延伸至AI眼鏡 、 無人機 、 機器人 、 智慧家庭及其他IoT設備 。
隨終端裝置導入更高速、更低延遲的無線連接規格，對PA輸出功率、線性
度與效率的要求同步提升，使 GaAs需求不再僅與智慧型手機銷售高度連
動，而是逐步擴展至更廣泛的連網設備市場。

全新為全球 最大砷化鎵磊晶廠 ，
近期受光通訊需求增加，光電子
產品占比逐步提升。

手機終端需求成長放緩，然規格
升級及終端應用增加，帶動 GaAs
需求提升。

### Page 3

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 3

下一世代WiFi 8則有望延續此一規格升級趨勢。相較過去WiFi世代主要追
求傳輸速度提升，WiFi 8進一步強調低延遲、穩定連線、長距離覆蓋與更
高上行流量(uplink)效能，應用場景也更貼近AI Edge Device、機器人及
即時互聯設備。整體而言，GaAs微電子產業的成長邏輯由手機出貨成長轉
向單機RF價值提升及連網裝置數量增加。

(2)  AI光互連升級推升InP需求，成為Ⅲ-V族主要成長動能 
生成式AI帶動CSP持續擴大資料中心投資，隨GPU與ASIC叢集規模增加，光
模組規格由400G快速升級至800G、1.6T，並朝3.2T發展，使高速PD、EML及
CW Laser 需 求 同 步 提 升 。LightCounting 於2026/7 最 新 預 估 ，2026 年
Ethernet光收發模組銷售額將年增73%，並預估2031年市場規模達800億美
元，反映AI高速光互連需求仍處於快速成長階段。

隨單通道速率由100G提升至200G， 接收端PD對頻寬要求提高 ， 發射端Laser
亦朝更高功率與高速率升級，使InP成為高速光通訊的關鍵材料。另一方
面，SiPh滲透率提升並不會取代InP， 因矽本身無法有效發光 ， 仍須搭配InP 
CW Laser 作為光源；後續 NPO/CPO 導 入 亦 將 延 續 外置 光 源 需 求 。同時
LightCounting指出在近期需求再度上修後，原先預期緩解的InP Laser短
缺可能延續至2027年中，顯示InP需求增速仍高於供給擴張。

(3) 上游原料高度集中中國，地緣政治加劇Ⅲ-V族供給限制 
Ⅲ-V族上游原料供給高度集中於中國，其中全球約98%的Gallium產量來自
中國 ，InP所需Indium亦約70%由中國供應 ， 使GaAs與InP供應鏈對中國政策
高度敏感 。 隨中美科技競爭升溫 ， 中國自2023年起對Gallium及相關化合物
實施出口許可，2025年再將InP基板納入出口管制，目前GaAs、Ge及InP基
板出口均須取得中國商務部許可，部分產品供貨時程已受到審批進度影
響。因此，原料供應高度集中與出口管制已成為影響Ⅲ-V族材料供應穩定
性及出貨時程的重要變數。

營運回顧及展望分析 
1. 2Q26 本業穩健改善，匯損低基期推升獲利大幅成長 
全新2Q26營收達9.65億元(+0.6%QoQ，+35.2%YoY)， 創單季歷史新高 ， 主要
受惠AI資料中心相關光電子需求持續成長，以及 Wi-Fi等微電子需求維持
穩健。毛利率為38.61%(+0.5ppts QoQ，+4.2ppts YoY)，主要受惠產品漲
價轉嫁基板及原物料成本，加上高毛利光電子產品占比提升。營業費用達
1.49億元(+7.3%QoQ，+20.6%YoY)、業外利益達0.13億元 ， 較去年同期業外
損失0.98億元大幅改善(2Q25受到新台幣急升造成匯兌損失)，使2Q26稅後
淨 利 達2.14 億元(+9.5%QoQ ，+488.3%YoY) ，EPS 為1.15 元(+8.5%QoQ ，
+475%YoY)。2Q26產品組合方面：微電子73%、光電子27%；相較1Q26微電子
75%、光電子25%，光電子營收占比持續提升。

光互連高速成長，接收發射端皆
需要 InP 關鍵材料。

鎵及銦原物料供應主要來自中國，
地緣政治成為供貨穩定的變數。

受惠於光電子需求持續成長且佔
比提升，營收創歷史新高且毛利
率季增年增。

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 4

2. 基板到位推升光電子放量，3Q26 營收獲利可望再創高 
全新 7 月 自 結 ， 營 收 達4.22 億元 (+45.11%YoY) 、 稅 後 淨 利1.15 億元
(+236.97%YoY)、EPS為0.62元 。 研究部以過往匯兌損益以及費用率去預估 ，
毛利率約為42%， 優於2Q26的38.6%， 受惠高毛利光電子產品比重提升 。8月
營收達4.71億元(+11.6%MoM，+71.2%YoY)， 累計3Q26達成率為67%， 符合研
究 部 預 期 。 產 品 組 合 方 面 ， 微 電 子 53.9%(-6ppts MoM) ，光電子
46.1%(+6ppts MoM)，光電子佔比提升受惠InP基板出口許可陸續取得、基
板供應改善 ， 以及客戶自帶基板增加 ； 然微電子進入3Q傳統手機新品旺季 ，
亦使營收穩健成長。研究部預期在光通訊需求高速成長下，預期4Q26有機
會超越微電子產品形成交叉，進而帶動整體毛利率。研究部預估3Q26營收
為13.92億元，EPS為1.69元。

圖二、全新產品組合佔比

資料來源：全新，統一投顧整理

3. 200G PD 1Q27 放量，CW Laser、EML 接續推升發射端成長  
接收端方面，目前新增PD專案以200G為主，美系大客戶已進入量產。目前
200G PD 占比仍低，但美系客戶客供料已陸續進廠，預計1Q27開始明顯放
量，屆時200G PD有機會占整體PD營收50%以上。200G PD技術門檻及ASP均
明顯優於100G，因此除帶動營收成長外，產品組合亦將持續往高階移動。
發射端方面，CW Laser目前已進入量產並開始出貨，2026年仍以小量出貨
為主 ， 預計2027年隨既有客戶需求提升進一步放量 。 此外 ， 目前已有陸系 、
台系及日系等多家客戶合作 ， 公司亦正與美系大客戶洽談新案 ；EML目前在
客戶驗證階段，預計於1H27開始放量，將成為發射端新增營收來源。管理
層預期2027年隨1.6T需求成長、CW Laser 及EML出貨規模擴大，以及發射
端ASP相較接收端1.5倍以上，發射端營收有望超越接收端，成為光電子下
一階段主要成長來源。

7 月及 8 月高毛利光電子營收佔
比提升，3Q26 獲利有望創高。

2027 年發射端營收佔比有望超越
接收端，成為光電子產品下一階
段成長動能。

4. 既有產能彈性轉向 InP，新增機台支撐 2027 光電子成長 
全新目前共有67台MOCVD，其中64台可於GaAs及InP間彈性共用、3台為GaN
專用設備 。7月營收雖創歷史新高 ， 但產能利用率仍僅50%以上，顯示MOCVD
設備本身具有餘裕， 現階段限制光電子出貨的主要因素仍為 InP基板供應
限制。 由於GaAs與InP共用機台轉換僅需3–5天 ， 若2027年基板供應持續改
善，公司可依訂單需求及產品毛利率，逐步將部分低毛利微電子產能轉向
PD、CW Laser、EML等高毛利InP光電子產品。近期，公司因應需求再新增
2台MOCVD供InP產能使用 ， 預計3Q27到廠 ， 屆時總機台數將由67台提升至69
台。

需求端方面，Lumentum持續擴充產能，但供給仍然落後客戶需求，供需缺
口由原先的25-30%擴大至30%以上。因此研究部認為，在磷化銦基板供應逐
步改善以及供需吃緊下，全新有望將既有GaAs/InP共用機台產能轉換，逐
步轉向高單價、高毛利光電子產品。綜上所述，研究部保守假設2027年部
分GaAs產能轉為InP使用 ， 搭配新增2台InP設備貢獻 ， 預估2027年光電子營
收年增138%。

投資建議 
產能不是問題，基板供應改善推升營收獲利成長

研究部初次給予全新買進建議 ， 主因為 ：(1)InP 基板供應改善帶動光電子
快速放量 ， 且高毛利光電子占比由1H26 約26%提升至7 月40%、8 月46.1%，
光電子超越微電子的黃金交叉將近 ；(2)產品組合轉佳 、較高毛利的客供料
占比逐步提升及價格調整效益帶動獲利改善，管理層預期 3Q26 毛利率可
回升至 40%以上；(3)展望 2027 年，美系客戶200G PD 預計 1Q27 開始明顯
放量，CW Laser 及 EML 發射端則接續放量，管理層預期 2027 年發射端營
收 有 望 超 越 接 收 端 。 綜 上 所 述 ， 研 究 部 預 估 全 新2026/2027 營收為
49.02/78.77 億元，分別年增 45%/60.7%，EPS 分別為 6.08/11.63 元，評
價方面，考量目前上游磊晶需求持續強勁，且基板供應逐步改善有望帶動
營收獲利成長，因此給予 60 倍本益比，目標價 700 元(60PER*2027EPS)。

2027 年MOCVD 總台數共 69 台，66
台為 GaAs 及 InP 共用 ， 後續可依
需求轉換成 InP 產能。

⚫ 在手訂單充沛， 2027 營收成長能見度高  
⚫ 半導體需求強勁 ，產能同步擴張  
⚫ 機器人由小量走向量產，長期成長空間可期  
在手訂單充沛， 2027 營收成長能見度高 ：盟立 2025 年營收 66.81 億
元，毛利率 18.8% ，EPS -0.8 元，近年持續投入新客戶 與 新 專 案 開 發，
為後續擴產需求預作布局。進入 2026 年後營運逐步轉強， 2Q26 營收
21.69 億元 (QoQ +16% 、YoY +7.9%)， 毛利率 22.2%，EPS 0.42 元，1H26
轉虧為盈，主要受惠先進封裝擴產，帶動 OHT、AGV 及 Stocker 等產
品成長。截至 8 月累計接單約 140 億元，其中半導體占 75% 、智能物
流 10%~15% ，年底訂單有望接近 200 億元，且 75%~80% 可望於 2027
年底前完成， 預期今年營收呈現逐月、逐季提升趨勢 。 
半導體需求強勁，產能同步擴張 ：半導體仍是盟立未來最重要的成長
核心，公司目前聚焦先進封裝、高階載板及半導體自動化三大領域，
部分客戶擴產規劃已延伸至 2028~2029 年，部分載板客戶既有擴產仍
不足以滿足需求，持續尋找新廠房與土地。此外， 公司產品技術已具
備進入 Fab 的條件 ，若後續能成功由 OSAT 及先進封裝延伸至前段
Fab ，單一客戶需求量與設備價值將進一步放大。 產品面， 2027 年底
OHT 產能目標 2,500~3,000 台，對應月產能約 250 台，較先前月產能
100 台大幅成長 ；EFEM 產能目標 3,000 台，Stocker 500~800 台，AMR 
800~1,000 台， 整體 產能 規模 將明顯 放大 。 海外 市場 也是為 下一 階段
成長動能，目前有 9.3 億元訂單於海外執行。  
機器人由小量走向量產，長期成長空間可期 ：機器人方面，盟英進度
持續 推進， 目前北美客戶已開始小量出貨，年底諧波減速機產能目標
30 萬顆以上， 2027 年有望進一步提升至 80 萬顆。現階段仍處於量產
初期，固定成本、折舊與設備投入較高，產量需達到數十萬顆以上規
模才較容易跨過損平點。機器人 2026 年仍偏向小量貢獻， 2027 年開
始觀察損平與放量進度， 2028 年後 將成為第二成長曲線。 我們預估 盟
立 2026/2027 年營收達 98.5 /146.7 億元、EPS  2.51 /6.8 元，給予 「買進」
評等，目標價 238 元(2027 年 EPSx 35 倍)。 
風險因子： 半導體客戶擴產遞延 、機器人產品競爭加劇 。

個股報告

2

盟立成立於 1989 年，為台灣主要自動化 系統整合廠商之一，營運模式以「系
統整合 (SI)+ 自 有設 備 」為 核 心 ， 業務 涵 蓋半 導體 、 面 板 、物 流 及機 器人 等 領
域。近年公司策略明顯聚焦半導體自動化市場，主要產品包括空中走行式搬
送系統 (OHT) 、晶圓 /載 板 傳 送 設 備(EFEM) 、 晶 圓 儲 存 設 備(Stocker) 及 無 人 搬
運車 (AMR/AGV) 。 
生產據點方面，公司目前於竹科、銅鑼、中科及南科均設有生產基地，其中
竹科以機器人相關產品為主，銅鑼及南科負責 OHT ，中科則生產 Stocker 及
EFEM 。隨半導體客戶持續擴廠，公司近年積極進行產品規格標準化及生產流
程優化，包括 OHT 機種收斂、軌道規格統一，以及軟體平台模組化，有利後
續由專案型生產逐步轉向標準化、規模化製造，降低單位生產成本並提升交
付效率。我們認為，盟立在半導體自動化市場的核心競爭優勢，在於產品線
完整及軟硬體整合能力 。半導體廠內 OHT、EFEM、Stocker 及 AMR 之間需高
度整合，設備導入後具一定系統黏著度，其中 OHT、EFEM 與 AMR 等設備在
資訊串聯及搬運指令整合上具有較高連動性，有利盟立由單一設備進一步擴
大整體專案供應範圍。  
除半導體設備外，公司也積極布局機器人產業。旗下盟英科技主要投入諧波
減速機及關節模組，並透過 傲威科技向整機機器人領域延伸。 2026 年公司與
精確、敏實集團合資成立傲威科技，由盟立提供技術與產品、敏實集團負責
結構件，進一步強化機器人整機整合能力。  
圖表一、盟立產能配置

資料來源：盟立，中信投顧整理  
AI 伺服器、 HPC 及先進封裝需求持續成長，使全球半導體資本支出進入新一
輪擴張週期。本輪擴產範圍涵蓋晶圓代工、記憶體、先進封裝、封測及載板
等領域，同步帶動廠內自動化搬運設備需求。  
台積電 ，2026 年 Capex 已上修至 600 ～640 億美元 ，公司更宣布額外投資 1,000
億美元用以在美新建 4 座新廠 ；也規劃台灣未來數年將興建高達 13 座先進製
程及先進封裝廠房 。我們預估台積電 2026/2027/2028 年全球合計在建案量分
別達 16/16/5 座， 2026~2028 年 Capex 合計突破 2,000 億美元。

圖表二、 台積電各廠區興建時程表

封測產業進入擴產週期，受惠 AI 需求強勁及台積電先進封裝產能快速擴張，
部分後段封裝製程委外至 OSAT 廠，日月光、京元電、力成、矽格及南茂等
業者擴產規劃積極 。 對盟立而言 ， 封測與先進封裝產能擴張將同步帶動 OHT 、
Stocker 、AMR 等自動化搬運設備需求。  
目前盟立 OHT 於 OSAT 端已完成部分客戶認證並進入接單及專案執行階段；
前端 FAB 市場則持續推進驗證與導入 。 Stocker 已通過晶圓廠客戶驗證 ， EFEM
則受惠高階 ABF 載板客戶擴產需求，相關載板客戶建廠規劃已延伸至 2029
年。  
圖表 三、OSAT 廠擴廠進度整理

資料來源：各公司，中信投顧整理  
人形機器人目前仍處於由技術驗證走向量產的初期階段。人形機器人市場規
模 2025~2030 年 CAGR  47.1% ，2030 年市場規模估達 390 億美元，顯示中長
期產業具高度成長潛力。在人形機器人關鍵零組件中，諧波減速機主要應用
於機器人關節系統。單台人形機器人使用約 15~18 顆諧波減速機；新一代機
種可能增加至 20 餘顆。

## Document 064: 260918_mq_alchip

Source PDF: all report pdf/260918_mq_alchip.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-18
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Macquarie Equity Research
18 September 2026
Technology
Alchip Technologies
Growth acceleration under way
Key Points
• Alchip's 3nm AI ASIC project ramped in 2Q26 and has driven strong
HoH and YoY growth from 2H26.
• We believe the 3nm AI ASIC ramp-up and next-generation tape-out in
late-2026 are key catalysts for Alchip shares.
• While we do not forecast additional CSP projects, the company is
"highly confident" it can secure another major CSP customer.
Expect 100%+ sales CAGR in 2026/27
• We believe share-price upside could be driven by: 1) the successful
ramp of its key customer’s 3nm AI ASIC (shipments from June), 2)
success in winning the key customer’s next-generation projects (tape
out in late-2026), and 3) other key projects, including networking-related
projects that could help drive 2027 and beyond sales growth, along with
multiple ADAS projects (the first major project ramped up in 1Q26).
• We maintain our positive long-term view on ASIC industry growth,
driven by the continued outsourcing of IC design services to Asia,
supported by a large talent pool and lower costs, and by Alchip's strong
competitive position and track record. We are not concerned about
Alchip's key customer's agreements with other semiconductor peers.
We believe these agreements highlight the large, growing TAM for the
customer's AI ASIC and related ecosystem. We also note that Alchip's
largest customer has invested in Alchip stock twice.
• Company remains bullish through at least 2029: During its 2Q26
analyst meeting in August, the company reiterated that sales should be
"at least in line with AI ASIC industry growth through 2029." The company
also expressed confidence in 3nm AI ASIC sales from June 2026 and in
the successful tape-out of its next-generation AI ASIC project before
year-end.
Earnings changes: We raise our 2026E/2027E/2028E EPS rep by
9%/27%/20% to reflect the higher scale of AI ASIC projects.
Valuation: We raise our target price 4% to NT$6,784, based on a 26x 2028E
PER, from NT$6,542, based on a 30x 2028E PER. Our new multiple is back in
line with the historical average and to reflect high customer concentration.
Catalysts: Sustained revenue growth from June; successful tape-out of a
next-generation project in 2H26.
Investment Thesis and Recommendation
As a key AI beneficiary with a strong track record of successful leading-
edge project ramps, we continue to believe in Alchip's long-term growth
outlook and remain positive on AI ASIC industry growth. Link to prior
Alchip research.

### Page 2

Macquarie Equity Research Alchip Technologies
Key Risks to Investment Thesis
• Project ramp-up delays or failure to reach mass production,
resulting in lower-than-expected scale.
• Order reductions from key customers, including potential
impacts from US semiconductor regulations, leading to
sales or earnings misses.
• Failure to secure key customers' next-generation projects.
Company Description
Alchip is a IC design service companies that assists IC design

and system companies to complete chip designs. Their
revenues come in two main streams: 1) NRE (non-recurring
engineering) which is the service provided starting with the
back-end design of the semiconductor to tape out at foundry.
2) Turnkey occurs when Alchip assists customers in placing
wafer orders to foundries (TSMC) and delivering final wafers to
customers.

Key Quant Findings
The quant model currently holds a reasonably negative
view on Alchip Technologies. The strongest style exposure
is Quality, indicating this stock is likely to have a superior
and more stable underlying earnings stream. The weakest
style exposure is Price Momentum, indicating this stock
has had weak medium to long term returns which often
persist into the future.

Recommendation definitions Volatility index definition Financial definitions
Macquarie – Asia and USA
Outperform – expected return >10%
Neutral – expected return from -10% to +10%
Underperform – expected return <-10%
Macquarie – Australia/New Zealand
Outperform – expected return >10%
Neutral – expected return from 0% to 10%
Underperform – expected return <0%
During periods of share price volatility,
recommendations and target prices may
occasionally and temporarily be inconsistent

with the above definitions.
Recommendations – 12 months
12-month target – Expected share price
in 12 months
Valuation – The company's estimated
fair value share price based on the disclosed
valuation methodology
Note: Quant recommendations may differ
from Fundamental Analyst recommendations
This is calculated from the volatility of historical price
movements.
Very high – highest risk – Stock should be expected to
move up or down 60–100% in a year – investors should be

aware this stock is highly speculative.
High – stock should be expected to move up or down at
least 40–60% in a year – investors should be aware this
stock could be speculative.
Medium – stock should be expected to move up or down
at least 25–40% in a year.
Low – stock should be expected to move up or down at
least 15–25% in a year.
* Applicable to select stocks in Asia/Australia/NZ
Note: expected return is reflective of a Medium Volatility
stock and should be assumed to adjust proportionately

with volatility risk
All "Adjusted" data items have had the following
adjustments made:
Added back: goodwill amortisation, provision for
catastrophe reserves, IFRS derivatives & hedging, IFRS
impairments & IFRS interest expense
Excluded: non recurring items, asset revals, property revals,
appraisal value uplift, preference dividends & minority
interests
EPS = adjusted net profit / efpowa*
ROA = adjusted ebit / average total assets
ROA Banks/Insurance = adjusted net profit /average

total assets
ROE = adjusted net profit / average shareholders funds
Gross cashflow = adjusted net profit + depreciation
*equivalent fully paid ordinary weighted average number of
shares
All Reported numbers for Australian/NZ listed stocks are
modelled under IFRS (International Financial Reporting

ab 18 September 2026
Global Research
Nanya Technology
Pricing reset driving upside in 3Q
3Q26 tracking well ahead of consensus
Nanya Tech's July ’26 revs were up 49% MoM and August 2%. Combined, July+August
is equivalent to 80% of VA consensus 3Q revenue of NT$111bn. We understand this
comes from both DRAM bit shipments and ASP upside. We now forecast QoQ bits up
5% vs -8% prior. More noticeably, we now forecast DRAM ASPs up 50% QoQ (vs.
+32% prior). We believe this comes from some 6-month to 1-year contracts which re-

set last July, leading to well above average QoQ pricing momentum. Also, with niche
DRAM exposure and less concentration around large clients, Nanya’s DRAM ASPs are
edging higher than its much larger peers as the upcycle continues. Looking into 4Q26,
we forecast ASPs to increase 10% QoQ. With that, gross margin should improve in
3Q26 to 86.1% from 79.5% in 2Q, and remain at elevated levels throughout 2027.
Reiterate Buy.
Not fulfilling customers’ demand until the next fab ramps

Nanya sees 50-60% fulfillment rate for demand currently. Several large customers are
not getting any allocation. Long term agreements cover >60% of bits already. Its new
fab will start equipment moving-in in 1Q27, with initial output in 3Q27. Initial capacity
will reach 30k wpm by 2028, with total theoretical capacity of 45-50k wpm. Nanya is
also constructing an EUV litho building with equipment moving in in 1Q28. The goal is
to secure 2 tools with space up to 5. Up to its 1d nm (which is 1a nm equivalent), Nanya

does not require EUV litho.
Increasing forecasts
We increase 3Q26E revs/EPS estimates to NT$133bn/NT$26.44 (by 30%/40%),
20%/37% above Visible Alpha consensus of NT$110bn/NT$19.26. We also increase
2026E/2027E/2028E EPS forecasts by 23%/12%/18% respectively on higher
DRAM ASP assumptions (9%/9%/4% higher per Gb vs prior).
Valuation: PT to NT$640 from NT$555; maintain Buy
We value Nanya Tech shares at 3.16x NTM P/BV (was 3.01x) based on our LT
ROE estimate of 26.5% (was 25.5%) and CoE of 8.4% (was 8.5%).

### Page 2

Nanya Technology 18 September 2026 ab 2
Nanya Technology UBS ResearchNanya Tech (2408.TW)
Thesis Map     UBS                    Research     THESIS MAP a guide to our thinking and what´s where in this report
Q: Will DRAM undersupply continue into 2028?
Yes in our view. Agentic AI is boosting further memory demand on multiple fronts beyond HBM,
including DDR5/LPDDR5 for conventional servers and CPU head nodes in AI servers. We now forecast
an acceleration of memory demand growth into 2027 with DRAM bit end-consumption growing

40% YoY vs 21% in 2026. As almost all of the incremental wafer capacity addition for DRAM is
going to HBM, supply is not catching up with demand. The main risk remains affordability by
customers as memory industry revenues are expected to reach US$1.64tn in 2027E.
Q: Can NTC grow as a niche DRAM player?
Yes. 2026 and 2027 are likely to be years of more constrained growth. Subsequently though, we
think its process tech migration to 1c nm and 1d nm will likely lead to a 30% increase in bits per wafer

for each process tech migration (at comparable yields). NTC is also slightly pulling in its new fab ramp,
with equipment expected to move in 1Q27. We also see a tail of DDR3 and DDR4 products NTC can
address for a while.
Nanya Tech shares trade at 2.59x NTM P/BV, above +1SD of its 10-year average. That said, we believe
the strength of the unfolding DRAM cycle as well as the tail of legacy products that Nanya can
address as a niche player should continue to provide support for the stock.

Our industry checks suggest HBM demand continues to result in capacity allocation away from DDR,
which may moderate possible cyclical corrections, supporting upside to DDR contract pricing for
longer.
At 2.59x NTM P/BV, we believe Nanya Tech shares are discounting a LT average ROE of 21.7% vs our
LT estimate of 26.5%.

Company Description
Nanya Tech is a pure DRAM company based in Taiwan, focusing on legacy products for
consumers, broad-based applications and is now growing in servers.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Nanya Tech is a pure-play DRAM manufacturer based in Taiwan mainly focused on legacy DDR

products for customers across various end applications. We think downside risks to our thesis
could include a potential slowdown in end demand and hence the conventional memory
cycle as well as Nanya's legacy-centric product portfolio. We value Nanya Tech shares using a
target NTM P/BV multiple based on long-term average ROE and cost of equity.

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over
the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

would be consistent with its investment objective and other principal investment strategies. The returns on a portfolio incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing
considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the
investment opportunities available to such portfolios may differ.

### Page 1

ab 18 September 2026
Global Research
First Read
Wiwynn
Reaffirm the strong   growth through 2027
New platforms support AI strength into 2027 while general server slower
before next ramp in 2027
We maintain our Buy on Wiwynn with a price target of $ 2,470  and also in the
UBS regional most preferred tech list. Amid the market noise, we stay positive on the
company's growth, with catalysts from liquid cooled ASIC servers in initial production
and ramping up further through 2027 and Helios racks on track as a new project

commencing from late 2026E through 2027. We do see general server cooling into Sep
and Q426 to offset the ASIC server ramp before picking up from Q127 as customers
transition to the next-generation Clearwater Forest/Venice platforms.
August sales ahead supported by general server growth
Wiwynn August sales of NT$144.3bn, +22.6% MoM, 50.4% YoY tracked ahead at
74%/73% UBSe/Street of +27%/+29% QoQ estimate in Q3. We believe the growth is
driven mainly driven by pre-build ahead of further price hikes. The company is seeing

tight supply across major component includes memory, CPU and PCB, but the impact on
deliveries has been limited through August. Since Q226, the company has booked
general server memory on its balance sheet rather than through sales and COGs, which
lifted gross margin in Q2. We factor in lower NRE and mix shifting from general server
now the highest margin with the consigned memory toward ASIC to lower GM/OpM off
the higher Q2 base of 9.3/7.3% to 8.8/6.8% in Q3 and 8.7/6.7% in Q4.

Multiple drivers support another strong year in 2027
We maintain 2027 revenue growth +89% YoY to NT$2,491bn, reflecting strength
across ASICs further ramping and rising liquid penetration toward 30% this generation,
GPUs with Helios racks ramping to 6-7k and general server continuing double digit
volume growth. We keep more conservative on Vera Rubin following limited shipments
on GB300 although should begin shipping in small volumes in Q426 and contributing

~2% of sales in 2027.
Reiterate our BUY with  price target of NT$2,470 (7,350 pre-split)
Following the recent 3-1 stock split, all per-share data below are on a post-split basis. We
reiterate our Buy rating and keep our basic EPS of NT$226.4/NT$174.1 reflecting better
margins, intact ASIC and general server ramps and customer AMD project from Q4 into
2027. Our PT of NT$2,470 is based on same 14x P/E on 2027E EPS also with 3% cash
yield (NT$74 cash dividend). We maintain a positive view reflecting Wiwynn's leverage

to ASIC servers, AMD racks, alongside healthy general server demand.

Company Description
Spun off from Wistron in March 2012, Wiwynn designs and manufactures cloud
infrastructure equipment for hyperscaler data centres. Prior to 2015, Wiwynn provided
storage servers and traditional servers. In 2015, Wiwynn began to shift its focus to

manufacturing full rack server for hyperscale data centre clients. Its key clients include US
Facebook (50-60% of total revenue), Microsoft (40-50%) and Amazon. Wiwynn is the
second-largest white-box server manufacturer in terms of shipments. It has production sites
in Mexico, the Czech Republic and China.
UBS EPS and Consensus EPS
The UBS EPS is an adjusted diluted EPS metric. It is calculated using the UBS analyst's
interpretation of earnings suitable for valuation purposes divided by the diluted number of

shares. This may differ to the way the consensus EPS metric has been calculated.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Our price target is based on PE multiple methodology.
Key downside risks for Wiwynn include: 1) a slowdown in the US CSP capex cycle; 2) margin
compression due to intense competition; and 3) unfavourable FX movement.

Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

## Document 077: 260922_ms_eink

### Page 1

M
Update
E Ink Holdings Inc.  | Asia Pacific
Read-across from Vusion's 2Q26
Earnings Call
Positive implications for E Ink from Vusion's comments on strong
order entries into 2H26 with further acceleration in 4Q26. But
with no strong catalyst in sight, we stay EW despite trough level
valuations.
What's new:
• VusionGroup (VU.PA, Not Covered), one of the major system integrators for
electronic shelf label (ESL) systems, reported its 1H26 audited results (see

here for read-across from its 2Q26 sales).
• By region, growth in 1H26 was mostly driven by America and improving
momentum in Europe. For EMEA, momentum was good with revenue and
order intakes growing. Management expects higher growth and more project
wins in 2H26, in the grocery sector and other verticals, and targets 15-20% of
growth in 2H26. For Americas, it sees strong pipeline with major North and
South American retailers, thus also anticipates  good momentum into 2H26

and strong future adoption in coming years.
• Management reiterated its 2026 guidance of 15-20% YoY (excluding the FX
and tariff impacts). It aims to grow VAS revenue by ~ 40% YoY to reach
~20% of its total revenue in 2026 (guided to rise to 30% in 2027).
• On order entries, Vusion management targets over €1bn in 2H26, with new
flows increasing over the coming months leading to a particularly strong
4Q26. It believes these could further fuel growth into 2027.

• It maintained the sales target of €2.2bn for 2027, driven by existing/new
customers, new solutions and broadened geographies. In the longer term, it
believes topline growth will be driven by increasing penetration of ESL (from
20% now to 50%) and VAS (which is more under-penetrated vs. ESL).  This is
despite the strong revenue growth in 2025-26 pulling in some momentum
from 2028-29.
Our view:
• Positive comments from Vusion on the robust order entries into 2H26 and

more deployment in the retail media are consistent with E Ink's view on the
solid ESL and signage demand for the year.
• That said, we think E Ink's CE business will remain a drag.
• Despite E Ink's trough valuation at 12x 2027e P/E and our belief in e-paper
demand in various applications, we see no strong catalysts in the near term,
and stay EW.

2
Valuation Methodology and Risks
E Ink Holdings Inc. (8069.TWO)
Base case, 12x 2027e P/E. We believe this valuation methodology best captures E Ink's earn-
ings growth momentum. We view our target multiple of 12x as fair, considering our estimated
17% earnings CAGR for 2025-28. The implied PEG of 0.7x is largely in line with its typical level
in 2022-25, when the stock was trading at an average P/E of 22x one-year forward consensus
EPS with a 37% earnings CAGR.

Risks to Upside
n Firmer-than-expected ESL orders
n Stronger demand for e-readers and e-notes
n Better macro conditions
n Milder competition
n Brisker inventory digestion
Risks to Downside
n Softer-than-expected ESL orders
n Slower demand for e-readers and e-notes
n Macro headwinds
n Intensified competition
n More protracted inventory digestion

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
Details: AT&S (ATSV.VI, not covered) has expanded its IC substrate agreement with
Marvell (MRVL.O, covered by Joseph Moore) to support AI and cloud infrastructure
demand, confirming Marvell as the previously unnamed anchor customer behind the
Kulim expansion alongside AMD (report).
The build-out spans the Plant 2 fit-out and a new substrate core and advanced

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
AT&S' announcement of further collaboration with Marvell again implies strong
demand for advanced ABF substrates.
It should be viewed primarily as customer identification and confirmation of the
strategic relationship behind the June project...
...rather than adding another expansion on top of June’s €1.5-2.0bn plan.
We believe this implies that Marvell is buying priority access to the part of the AI

supply chain most likely to gate its shipments.
This supports our positive view on the ABF segment. We think it has positive
implications for Unimicron and Nan Ya PCB, both of which we rate OW.
September 23, 2026 11:24 AM GMT

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Unimicron (3037.TW)
Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate
value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of 9.2%
[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and
a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 15%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected ABF substrate demand from PC and server customers

n Capex cuts; halt to its capacity expansion plan
n Continued yield issues of alternative technology that doesn't require substrate (e.g.,
CoWoP)
Risks to Downside
n Sudden demand shortfall
n Technological change that would not require ABF substrates
n Intensifying competition
n Yield issues or production hiccups when ramping new capacity
Nan Ya PCB (8046.TW)
Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate

value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of 9.3%
[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and
a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 17%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected ABF and BT demand
n Faster-than-expected pickup in AI and 5G demand
n Stronger-than-expected ASP hike
n Continued yield issues of alternative technology that doesn't require substrate (e.g.

CoWoP)
Risks to Downside
n Sudden demand shortfall, creating headwinds for ABF substrate demand and pricing
n Technological change that would not require ABF substrates
n Intensifying competition

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

### Page 1

Formosa Petrochemical (6505.TW)
Upgrade to Buy: Superior Margin on Tanker Fleet + High Export Ratio
CITI'S TAKE 
We upgrade FPCC from Neutral to Buy reflecting its unique exposure with 
in-house VLCC shipping fleet covering ~60-70% of oil transportation (least 
impacted by recent spot freight rate spike) and highest diesel export ratio 
( ~85% vs. 60% for S-Oil/SKI and 5-10% for TOP/SPRC). We see manageable 
impact from CPC’s pump price freeze (minimal margin squeeze at ~$80/bbl 
Dubai) while FPCC is also a beneficiary of any potential US diesel export 
restriction (Iink). We expect FPCC to offer the highest 26-27E dividend yield 
( ~ 7-8%) among 4 Formosa sisters and a premium valuation seems justified 
vs. Asian peers. We think oil product tightness might take longer to resolve 
given extensive Russia refinery outages. New TP at NT$108 (from NT$40). 
Quantifying impact of domestic price cap — Taiwan’s CPC indicates its retail price 
absorption reached NT$22bn cumulative YTD (Fig.1) vs. NT$17bn in end-Jun and we 
estimate ~NT$7bn in Sep alone (similar to Mar peak). FPCC has 22% Taiwan market 
share and 2025 domestic gasoline & diesel volume was 35k/17k b/d or 12% of CDU 
runs subject to price control (vs. ~18% for S-Oil/SKI, 35% for Thai peers). Assuming 
oil price stays at $100 until end-4Q, we estimate 2H26 domestic forgone EBIT could 
be similar to 1H ( ~NT$5bn) or ~US$2/bbl overall GRM drag. But this was more than 
compensated by SG product crack strength with 2025 gasoline/diesel/jet export 
ratios of 59%/86%/99%, respectively.   
Buffered from current $20+ spot VLCC rate — FPCC is one of the very few refiners 
that owns vessels (through 20% stake in Formosa Plastics Marine that has 6 VLCCs). 
We expect it could enjoy stable freight cost under LT time chartering. We also see 
lower concern on crude sourcing from SoH disruption in 3Q noting Jul/Aug refinery 
runs were 88%/92%. But we see pullback into 4Q (mid-70%) on maintenance. 
Improved olefin competitiveness from 2027 — We expect FPCC could raise cracker 
utilisation next year (33% average since 2Q) after partial conversion to ethane feed 
(up to ~400kt pa usage or ~20% of output) with minimal capex. 
Implications — Our revised TP of NT$108 is based on historical peak 2.3x P/BV (core 
book) at ~50% premium to Korean peer S-Oil given limited free float and MTM for 
affiliates (its 7.9% stake in Nanya Tech is worth NT$14/share based on market price). 
Our sensitivity analysis suggests a $5 diesel crack rise vs. our mid-$30/bbl base 
case will raise FY27E EPS by ~10%. 
n

3
In Taiwan, state-owned Chinese Petroleum Corp (CPC) announces retail price
adjustment on a weekly basis based on government’s fuel pricing formula (linked to
70% Dubai / 30% Brent crude basket adjusted for exchange rate) but with higher
absorption amount as oil goes higher. FPCC tends to follow suit. CPC’s domestic
price absorption was minimal in Jun-Jul’26 when Dubai price falls to ~$80/bbl or

below. However, there was a price freeze last week despite crude basket surged
another $10/bbl WoW to $126/bbl.

Bull/Bear: Formosa Petrochemical (6505.TW)

### Page 7

Formosa Petrochemical (6505.TW)
25 September 2026 Citi Research
7
Formosa Petrochemical
Company description
Formosa Petrochemical (FPCC) is part of the Formosa Group in Taiwan. It
owns a 540,000 b/d refinery, 2.9m tonnes of ethylene capacity from three
naphtha crackers, and a 2,800MW cogeneration power plant. It was set up
when the Taiwan government approved expansion of the private sector into
the oil refining and upstream chemical business.
Investment strategy

We rate Formosa Petrochemical stock a Buy. Despite a likely gradual GRM
normalisation ahead (dependent on Middle East / Russia supply disruptions),
we expect oil product tightness might take longer to resolve vs. crude oil
market. Its gasoline/diesel export ratio was the highest among Asian peers,
which should more than buffer margin squeeze from domestic pump control.
FPCC was the least impacted by recent spot freight rate spike given in-house
VLCC shipping fleet. While we remain concerned about an L-shaped ethylene

cycle in the next 2-3 years, FPCC could enjoy better cost competitiveness as
~20% of olefin cracker feedstock will shift to ethane from early-2027 with
minimal capex. The LT refining outlook could remain solid due to permanent
closures in the US/Europe. Healthy dividend yield (likely to be the highest
among 4 Formosa sisters in FY26E), stable utility earnings and the stock's
relatively low free float (15%) vs. its peers could provide further support.

Valuation
Our NT$108 target price for Formosa Petrochemical is based on historical
peak-cycle 2.3x FY27E P/BV with adjusted book value excluding valuation
gain of affiliates, ~50% premium to Korean peer S-Oil implied from our
TP given limited free float and MTM for affiliates. We use P/BV as our
valuation methodology due to the asset-heavy nature of the business, and
factor in its scarcity premium vs, regional peers as the only energy play in
Taiwan.

Risks
Key downside risks that could impede the shares from reaching our target
price include: 1) weaker-than-expected oil and/or chemical demand; and 2)
Taiwan government intervention on CPC's oil products pricing mechanism.
Key upside risks are: 1) extended refinery outages in the Middle East / Russia,
2) stronger global oil demand growth; 3) robust chemical re-stocking in
China; and 4) a short-term spike in refining margins on any unscheduled
shutdowns or for weather-related reasons (e.g., cold winter).

Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

AVC (3017.TW): Liquid cooling rising adoption along with new AI servers 
across GPU and ASIC to drive sequential monthly revenues growth; Buy
25 September 2026 | 7:51PM HKT
3-month revenues preview: We expect AVC’s monthly revenues to continue 
sequential growth through Dec 2026, leading 3Q26 / 4Q26 revenues to grow at 
+18% / +27% QoQ, driven by (1) new AI server racks ramp up, driving overall liquid 
cooling penetration rate. As we highlighted in June (report link), we expect AI server 
rack (NVL72-equivalent) shipment (incl. Nvidia and AMD) to reach 55k/ 105k/ 163k 
in 2026-28E, increasing by 16% / 20% in 2027 / 28E, reflecting our positive view on 
AI server racks, (2) its major ASIC AI server customer’s new model ramp up, driving its 
air-cooling and liquid-cooling components, along with a rising liquid cooling 
penetration rate, (3) its customer base expansion to a new ASIC AI server, to further 
drive its liquid cooling components in 4Q26, and (4) increasing adoption rate of 
ultra-thin VC in consumer electronics to fit the lightweight design and major 
customer’s foldable phone model. We expect AVC’s net income to grow at a +39% 
CAGR in 2026-28E, riding on the strong AI spending, a growing liquid cooling 
penetration rate, and AVC’s leading market position in total solutions (air cooling, 
liquid cooling with various components / systems). Maintain Buy.

Earnings revisions: We factor in AVC’s Aug revenue and raise our 2026-28E net

Price Target Risks and Methodology - AVC 
We are Buy-rated on AVC. Our 12-month target price for AVC is based on a near-term 
P/E, consistent with our Taiwan Technology coverage. We base our target price of 
NT$4,513 on a target P/E multiple of 30.3x applied to our forward-year EPS (2027E). 
Our target P/E is derived from the correlation between P/E and EPS growth of its peers 
in technology hardware.  
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected adoption of liquid cooling; (2) 
lower-than-expected demand for AI servers; (3) fiercer-than-expected competition; 
and (4) a slower-than-expected recovery in general servers.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

Idea
Current debate: Can MediaTek meet Street 2028 forecasts given Intel EMIB-T
supply issue? Intel's EMIB-T substrate supply has been a potential bottleneck for
future TPU v9 ("Humufish") shipments in 2028, making some investors reluctant to
own MediaTek. The issue stems from a supply-demand mismatch. We forecast 3mn
TPU v9 units, as demand from Google (covered by Brian Nowak) remains strong.
However, as discussed in our AI Supply Chain Tracker, we expect only 2mn EMIB-T

substrates, i.e. 40–45k wafer-equivalent EMIB-T capacity in 2028. This aligns with
Digitimes' report that Google is considering CoWoS as a backup for its 2027 TPU on
lagging EMIB-T substrate yields.  For TSMC, supporting CoWoS could also help
regain market share from EMIB-T. Our checks indicate that both MediaTek and
TSMC are engaging to meet customer (Google) demand.
TSMC's willingness to support production increases confidence in our aggressive

2028 EPS forecast: Assuming TSMC CoWoS-L can support 0.5mn TPU v9 units and
EMIB-T can support 2.5mn units, our 3mn-unit target for 2028 appears highly
achievable, with a higher ASP from the CoWoS version. CoWoS-L carries a
structurally higher cost, and the RDL (redistribution layer) interposer connection
requires tape-out of three additional photomask layers. However, our checks also
suggest that Google is willing to pay these incremental costs to secure dual

sourcing, which should help MediaTek sustain its gross margin.
New debate: Who could be the second CSP customer? Investors have asked
whether MediaTek could win Tesla's AI6 edge AI chip program. We believe AI6 is
better suited to GUC, given its 10%–25% margin profile. Considering the higher tech
content of MediaTek's design services, incl. IP + design capabilities, we believe
MediaTek targets a 30%–35% margin profile. Instead, we see a growing probability

that MediaTek wins a cloud AI ASIC project from an AI-focused CSP and/or AI lab
customer through its  Nvidia / NVLink Fusion partnership. In previous notes, we
highlighted interest from both SpaceX and an AI lab customer. The customer's
decision could come in 4Q26 and serve as a significant catalyst for MediaTek. With
TPU upside and a potential new project, we expect MediaTek AI ASIC-related
revenue of US$13.5bn and US$54bn in 2027 and 2028, resp., representing c.38% and

72% of total revenue. Reiterate Top Pick.

### Page 2

M
Idea
2
TSMC CoWoS capacity support eases concerns regarding
MediaTek 2028  TPU production
Smartphone market still lukewarm into 2027, but can serve as a long-term option
value: At our recent China Best Conference, meetings involving China smartphone OEM
and NAND module makers appeared to indicate China's smartphone demand may not
recover in 2027, as mobile DRAM prices may not be falling anytime soon. We expect
MediaTek’s 3Q26 revenue to be ahead of guidance because of customer pull-in to avoid

the SoC price hike rather than due to demand improvement.
That said, once AI smartphones become a killer application, MediaTek should rank among
the best-positioned semiconductor companies to provide the edge computing capabilities
required in handsets. We also believe 2028 should be a strong year for MediaTek's
smartphone SoC business.
1. Google TPU supply chain update
TSMC CoWoS may  support both 3nm (Zebrafish) and 2nm (Humufish) MediaTek TPU
in 2028: In our earlier report, MediaTek: 2Q26 results preview: Smartphone weak, but

Cloud AI taking off; OW (26 Jul 2026), we noted that  MediaTek may secure a portion of
CoWoS capacity for 2nm TPU to ensure minimum volume production. But, we believe it
will use EMIB-T from Intel (covered by Joseph Moore)  as the primary solution to reduce
costs and scale volume in 2028, if execution proceeds well. Our recent industry checks
suggest TSMC CoWoS would continue to support both 3nm (Zebrafish) and 2nm
(Humufish) MediaTek TPU in 2028. We now assume around 500k units of 2nm TPU going

through CoWoS production as a second source. The 3nm TPU in 2028 of around 2mn
units is higher than our initial expectation of 1mn units. We therefore raise our MediaTek
2028 TPU revenue assumption to US$51.5bn.
2nm TPU TAM is supported by different SKU: At our AI Summit, MediaTek management
stated that its design service is capable of both training and inference design. Recently,
investors have been asking whether MediaTek is involved in the design of 2nm TriggerFish

TPU, intended for AI inference. Our supply chain checks suggest this could be true. The
TriggerFish chip has more features (I/O die upgrade, one more simulation die, and change
of HBM controller to HBM4e), so we think MediaTek’s 2nm TPU blended ASP could go
higher. We estimate ASP at US$12-20k (we now model US$13k). But again, the EMIB-T
substrate yield should be the key risk to volume.
CoWoS constraint eased, but ABF substrate supply still the gating factor for TPU bull

case: In our recent AI supply chain tracker, we noted that MediaTek’s 180k CoWoS-S
booking implies ~3.6mn units of TPU 8t (ZebraFish) in 2027, above our original 3mn
shipment assumption. However, we continue to bake in some conservatism as we had
assumed ABF substrate shortages. So,  we still forecast 3mn units in 2027.

2. Who could be the second and third customer for MediaTek
in AI ASICs?
NVLink Fusion partnership should be the key catalyst for MediaTek to win new
projects:
Through the NVLink Fusion platform, MediaTek's XPU customers can leverage NVIDIA's
scale-up and scale-out networking technologies to build AI server systems.
In our report, MediaTek: NVIDIA and Alphabet Invest in MediaTek’s CB for Extensive
Partnership in ASIC; Stay OW, Top Pick (31 Aug 2026), we shared that MediaTek had

maintained its 2027 ASIC TAM and market share guidance despite the broader partnership,
noting that commercialization would take time. However, we believe the partnership
materially increases MediaTek's chances of winning a second CSP customer.
Overall, we see upside to our 2028 ASIC revenue assumptions, driven by: 1) a higher
probability of winning a second or even third CSP customer; 2) greater revenue from
material preparation; and 3) a strengthened partnership with Alphabet on TPU programs.

In today's research, we incorporate potential revenue contributions from new customers.
Exhibit 2: NVLink Fusion to support connection with custom ASIC
Source: Nvidia
MediaTek's second CSP customer could begin contributing revenue from subsystem
builds in 2028: We believe the subsystem would include procurement of key components
such as HBM and ABF substrates, as well as wafer foundry capacity prepayments.

### Page 4

M
Idea
4
Our checks suggest MediaTek may target subsystem builds for a second CSP customer
rather than supplying the XPU itself. We now expect an initial revenue contribution in
2028, based on approximately 100k subsystem units at an ASP of US$25k.
We therefore assume a US$2.5bn revenue contribution in 2028, based on 100k units and
an ASP of US$25k, including HBM chip consignment, known-good dies (KGDs) for
compute chips, and chiplets.
On margin trends, management highlighted continued value creation for customers across

both silicon and packaging design. As a result, we expect ASICs to remain accretive to
operating margins. At the gross margin level, management indicated that margins vary by
project, potentially reflecting support for full-rack systems. We model 2nm TPU gross
margins at c.35% (vs. c.40% for 3nm TPUs) given the larger revenue scale. We continue to
expect operating margins of ~20–25%, above the smartphone business unit's 15–20%.

3. Why TSMC is motivated  to support MediaTek and Google?
TSMC's CoWoS-L solution carries a structurally higher cost due to its larger reticle size, as
we discussed in The Foundry Floorplan: TSMC preview update; CoWoS vs. EMIB
competition (14 Apr 2026). We believe TSMC also wants to address growing competition
from Intel's EMIB-T, which we estimate will reach 40-45k wafer equivalent capacity 2028,

implying a 10-15% share of the 2.5D packaging market.
For MediaTek to adopt TSMC's CoWoS-L as a second source alongside EMIB-T, the RDL
(redistribution layer) connection to the interposer requires tape-out of three additional
photomask layers. However, our checks suggest that Google is willing to absorb the
incremental costs associated with dual sourcing, which should allow MediaTek to sustain
its gross margin.

Intel EMIB-T can support 2.5mn units of our 2028 TPUv9 shipment in

2028
Average EMIB-T capacity is around 5kwpm in 2026, which we expect to further scale to 15-
20kwpm in 2027 and 40-45kwpm in 2028. All of the capacity mentioned above is
converted for 12-inch wafer equivalent, which we believe would be much easier to

### Page 6

Idea
6
communicate with customers compared  with TSMC CoWoS capacity.
Although some small projects are ongoing, we still see MediaTek/Google Humufish
consuming most of the capacities during 2027-28. We now expect  Intel EMIB-T could
produce close to 2.5mn units of Humufish from late 2027-2028, by assuming 4-5 chips per
wafer of EMIB-T, while TSMC CoWoS could support around 500k units.
We see current average EMIB-M capacity being 110kwpm, which would slightly decrease

to 95kwpm in 2027 as Intel is converting some capacities toward EMIB-T.

8
Raise price target from NT$5,588 to NT$6,188
We continue to derive our price target (our base-case scenario value) from a residual
income model. The price target change mostly reflects our earnings estimate changes. All
of  our key valuation assumptions remain unchanged: cost of equity at 9.2% (beta of 1.2,
risk-free rate of 2%, and risk premium of 6%), intermediate growth rate of 12%, and
terminal growth rate of 3.0%.
Our bull- and bear-case scenario values rise from NT$7,675 to NT$8,500 and  NT$2,845 to

NT$3,150, respectively.

### Page 9

Morgan Stanley Research 9
Relative valuation
We view the P/E as relatively attractive – even after the share price rally – given strong AI
demand. Although MediaTek faces smartphone headwinds in 2026, we believe it is time to
look beyond 2026. We think this margin pressure is fully priced in, while TPU upside is
not. We believe the stock should trade more than +1SD above its historical average,
supported by strong TPU demand. Our  price target of NT$6,188 implies 42x  2027 EPS, on

our estimates.

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

## Document 094: 260928_ml_aspeed

Source PDF: all report pdf/260928_ml_aspeed.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-28
Pages: 9

### Page 1

Aspeed

Growth profile remains robust into 2027; 
reiterate Buy

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 26,000 TWD | Price: 19,835 TWD

Looming price hike and smoother supply 
Aspeed’s (see report) 4Q26 revenue guidance in our view should incorporate 20% price 
hike across the board including AST2700 BMC (priced at ~US$30 after the hike), mainly 
reflecting the cost inflation from OSAT (outsourced semiconductor assembly/testing) 
vendor. Yet with incremental supply secured across foundry, OSAT, substrate and glass 
cloth, the firm expects a normalized DoI (days of inventory) level of ~12 weeks from 
2Q27.   
Sustained leadership with value-add in security 
Management continues to underscore around 2-year technology lead vs its main 
competitor in the merchant market, while the adoption of Caliptra 2.2 standard in 
AST2750 BMC further enhanced its value-add in network security. We deem this a 
positive to (see report) eMemory given the share gain in PUF business against its 
counterpart in US, from which Aspeed adopts Caliptra 1.1 in AST2700 BMC.   
Rosy demand growth outlook for 2027 
Looking into 2027, Aspeed cites 50-60% growth for BMC applied to GPU and ASIC 
markets, while there could be 1) an even stronger growth in Agentic AI (artificial 
intelligence); and 2) 6-10% YoY in general server (w.o. Agentic AI in our view). Given such 
demand strength, the company may provide an updated version of the BMC TAM (total 
addressable market) by end-2026.   
Flag recent positives from the industry; re-it. Buy 
Recently we see positives to server CPU (central processing unit) supply chain from (see 
reports) Meta’s Muse and expert call takeaways which cite continuous share gain by 
ARM-based solutions. Reiterate Buy on Aspeed given its unrivalled position in a 
structurally growing market.

Key Metrics      
* For full definitions of iQmethod SM measures, see page 6.

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
Aspeed was founded in 2004 as a fabless company, 
focusing on the design of baseboard management 
controller (BMC), which could be used in remote servers. 
Aspeed has 70+% market share in BMC, while it also offers 
other products including PC/AV extension IC and digital 
image processors.

Investment Rationale 
We rate Aspeed as Buy, as we anticipate stable growth in 
the regular server market into 2026-27, along with its 
growing exposure to AI server market. Outside of server 
BMC, we also like its increasing traction in new products 
(mini-BMC, root of trust, etc.) in the server area. Further, we 
see stronger growth in non-server business led mainly by 
Cupola 360.

Stock Data  
Price to Book Value 60.5x

### Page 4

Price objective basis & risk 
Aspeed (XLKMF) 
Our DCF-based PO is NT$26,000, and we believe the valuation is justified by (1) the 
firm's high market share in the server BMC market, (2) secular growth in overall server 
market, despite near-term ebbs and flows of orders and shipments, (3) nearly 60% 
earnings CAGR in 2026-28E, and (4) about NT$215 net cash/share on hand, we assume 
WACC of 7% and a terminal growth rate of 3%.

Upside risk to our PO are 
(1) stronger-than-expected operating leverage on stronger revenue growth, and (2) 
faster progress in ramping up non-BMC product lines including Cupola 360.

Downside risks to our PO are 
(1) softer server (thus BMC) demand due to macro uncertainties, or pressured by trade 
conflict 
(2) market-share loss in BMC to current/potential competitors such as Nuvoton 
(3) customers' market-share loss in ones (such as HP and Dell) to which Aspeed has less 
exposure.

鈊象 (3293 TT)  Buy

2027 年因 Social SS 帶動，營收展望正向，給予 Buy 建議  
投資建議 ： 2027 年 TaDa Gaming 在美國發展 Social SS 已明顯
看到成效，因基期和市占率仍偏低，故 Social SS 會是未來 1~2
年營收的主要成長動能，考量 2027 年展望正向且評價偏低，給
予 Buy 的投資建議。  
美國的 Social SS 因基期和市占率仍偏低，為未來 1~2 年的主要
成長動能： 展望 2H26~2027 年，區域營收比重方面， 01~08/2026
為東南亞 54%，台灣 16%，中國 5%，美國 14%，其他 11%，若
與 01~02/2026 和 2025 年相比，台灣和中國比重下滑，美國和
其他比重提升，東南亞則持平。以實際營收來看， 01~08/2026 東
南亞仍有 YOY +18% 的成長，美國和其他地區則是 YOY +50%
以上的成長，台灣和中國則為 YOY 衰退。授權遊戲的部分，美
國和其他地區營收大幅成長，主要是 TaDa Gaming 在拓展美國
和中南美市場已明顯看到成效，歐洲和非洲則因為競爭激烈，
仍在努力推廣中。其中美國市場過去是以三大州為主，為過去
常講的 SS 或是美國授權，在三大州的市占率已達 60%，往上成
長空間有限。 TaDa Gaming 在美國發展的 Social SS 不只侷限在
三大洲，而是透過代理商在法規允許的 35 個州推廣，包括原來
的三大州，且可用手機遊玩， 2024 年開始積極推廣， 2H25 已有
一個月幾千萬元的營收貢獻， 01~08/2026 成長加速，加上基期
和市占率仍偏低，故公司樂觀表示，美國的 Social SS 會是未來
1~2 年公司的主要成長動能，美國的比重在未來會逐步提升。東
南亞授權的部分， 2H26~2027 年還是看好會持續成長，不過因
為基期已墊高，能新切入的區域及規模較小，成長會較
2023~2025 年明顯減緩。網路遊戲的部分，因為台灣同類型遊戲
眾多，競爭非常激烈， 2026 年預估營收為小幅下滑， 2027 年此
趨勢仍難以改變。商用遊戲機的部分，原本 03/2026 法說預估
2026 年營收 YOY 為小幅成長，不過因為中國景氣尚未明顯復
甦，許多遊樂場在整頓，造成許多中古機台流入市面，影響公
司新機的推廣，故修正為 2026 年營收小幅衰退。

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 9 月 24 日

2 群益投顧 
  ESG(Environment, Social, Governance) ：

公司簡介：  
鈊象 (3293 TT) 成立於 1989 年，製作商用電子遊戲機起家，較著名的有《黃
飛鴻》、《三國戰記》和《西遊釋厄傳》等等。隨著街機的式微， 2000 年新
增個人電腦遊戲的設計與研發，並成功開發出 《明星三缺一》 單機遊戲， 2004
年則開發連線撮合遊戲平台 Game 淘，該平台包含可連線對戰的《明星 3 缺
1 Online 》。 2007 年推出的《唯舞獨尊 Online 》亦是鈊象的主力遊戲之一。
2010 年後，智慧手機崛起加上網速的提升，帶動手遊蓬勃發展，鈊象也將
重心轉往手遊市場，推出 《金猴爺》 、 《明星三缺一手機版》 、 《滿貫大亨》 、
《金好運》、《海王寶藏》和《金虎爺》等博弈手遊。 2017 年開始經營美國
授權遊戲市場，採用授權給當地代理商的方式，收取授權金和服務費，經過
多年的經營，取得市佔第一的佳績。東南亞授權則是 2021 年開始布局，與
美國授權一樣只收取授權金和服務費，不同的地方在於：東南亞授權有很多
代理商，美國授權則有兩個代理商；東南亞授權經營了 1~1.5 年就看到成
效，美國授權則是經營了 4 年。 01~08/2026 營收比重的部分，網路遊戲佔
24% ，授權遊戲佔 74% ，商用遊戲機佔 2%。  
美國的 Social SS 因基期和市占率仍偏低，為未來 1~2 年的主要成長動能：
展望 2H26~2027 年，遊戲營收排名方面， 01~08/2026 營收排行榜為東南亞
授權、 TaDa Gaming 、 《明星三缺一》 、美國授權、 《金猴爺》 、 《金好運》 、
《滿貫大亨》、《海王寶藏》，該排名與 01~02/2026 一致。區域營收比重
方面， 01~08/2026 為東南亞 54% ，台灣 16% ，中國 5%，美國 14% ，其他
11% ，若與 01~02/2026 和 2025 年相比，台灣和中國比重下滑，美國和其他
比重提升，東南亞則持平。以實際營收來看， 01~08/2026 東南亞仍有 YOY 
+18% 的成長，美國和其他地區則是 YOY +50% 以上的成長，台灣和中國則
為 YOY 衰退。  
授權遊戲的部分，美國和其他地區營收大幅成長，主要是 TaDa Gaming 在
拓展美國和中南美市場已明顯看到成效，歐洲和非洲則因為競爭激烈，仍在
努力推廣中。其中美國市場過去是以三大州為主，為過去常講的 SS 或是美
國授權，在三大州的市占率已達 60%，往上成長空間有限。 TaDa Gaming 在

3 群益投顧 
美國發展的 Social SS 不只侷限在三大洲，而是透過代理商在法規允許的 35
個州推廣，包括原來的三大州，且可用手機遊玩， 2024 年開始積極推廣，
2H25 已有一個月幾千萬元的營收貢獻， 01~08/2026 成長加速，加上基期和
市占率仍偏低，故公司樂觀表示，美國的 Social SS 會是未來 1~2 年公司的
主要成長動能，美國的比重在未來會逐步提升。東南亞授權的部分，
2H26~2027 年還是看好會持續成長，不過因為基期已墊高，能新切入的區域
及規模較小，成長會較 2023~2025 年明顯減緩。  
網路遊戲的部分，因為台灣同類型遊戲眾多，競爭非常激烈， 2026 年預估
營收為小幅下滑， 2027 年此趨勢仍難以改變。  
商用遊戲機的部分，原本 03/2026 法說預估 2026 年營收 YOY 為小幅成長，
不過因為中國景氣尚未明顯復甦，許多遊樂場在整頓，造成許多中古機台流
入市面，影響公司新機的推廣，故修正為 2026 年營收小幅衰退。  
綜合以上，預估 2026 年營收為 264.33 億元， YOY +19.97% ；稅後純益為
134.05 億元， YOY +23.53% ；稅後 EPS 47.57 元。預估 2027 年營收為 320.76
億元， YOY +21.35% ；稅後純益為 167.54 億元， YOY +24.99% ；稅後 EPS 
59.45 元。

2027 年 TaDa Gaming 在美國發展 Social SS 已明顯看到成效，因基期和市占
率仍偏低，故 Social SS 會是未來 1~2 年營收的主要成長動能，考量 2027 年
展望正向且評價偏低，給予 Buy 的投資建議。
